- **hos-session-summary**: List the subjects a session took up, one line each, in the order it took them up — what the session was about, for a reader deciding where to look or which session this was. The reason the session was started is its main subject: it is found first, in the opening turns rather than in any summary of them, and every other line is held against it. Held to the session itself and read off its conversation. It lists subjects and carries no verdict, outcome or account of the work: whether the session may be exited belongs to the session-report convention. ([openreachtech/hora-skills-ort-support](https://github.com/openreachtech/hora-skills-ort-support/tree/HEAD/kit/skills/hos-session-summary) · ★32)
